## Summary: "Detect All Models" Flow for OpenAI-Compatible Providers

Based on my thorough exploration of the Moltis codebase, here's how the "Detect All Models" functionality works end-to-end:

### **1. Frontend Flow (JavaScript)**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/web/src/assets/js/page-providers.js`

- **Button Click Handler** (line 348): User clicks "Detect All Models" button, calls `runDetectAllModels()` function
- **Function** `runDetectAllModels()` (lines 117-151):
  - Calls `fetchModels()` and `fetchProviders()` to show current model list first
  - Calls RPC `models.detect_supported` with empty params for full detection
  - Listens to `models.updated` WebSocket events for real-time progress updates
  - Phases: `start`, `progress`, `complete`, `error`
  - Tracks progress with signals: `detectingModels`, `detectSummary`, `detectError`, `detectProgress`

- **Event Handler** `handleModelsUpdatedEvent()` (lines 41-67): Receives WebSocket broadcasts with detection phases and updates UI

### **2. Backend RPC Handler**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/gateway/src/methods/services.rs`

- **RPC Registration** (lines 3158-3169): Routes `"models.detect_supported"` to `ctx.state.services.model.detect_supported()`

### **3. Model Detection Service**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/chat/src/lib.rs`

- **Implementation** (lines 1883-2224): `LiveModelService::detect_supported()` async method
  - **Parameters**:
    - `background`: bool (defaults false for manual detection)
    - `reason`: string (e.g., "manual" or "provider_connected")
    - `maxParallel`: u64 (defaults 8, clamps 1-32)
    - `maxParallelPerProvider`: per-provider concurrency limit
    - `provider`: optional provider name filter
  
  - **Semaphore Gate**: Ensures only one detection run at a time (line 1902)
  
  - **Broadcast Phases** (lines 1925-2221):
    1. **catalog**: notify clients to refresh model list
    2. **start**: send initial stats (total models, 0 checked)
    3. **progress**: incremental progress updates
    4. **complete**: final summary with results
    5. **error**: on failure
  
  - **Model Filtering** (lines 1939-1957):
    - Gets all enabled (non-disabled) models from provider registry
    - Filters by provider if `provider_filter` is specified
    - Collects tuples of (model_id, display_name, provider_name, provider_trait_object)
  
  - **Concurrent Probing** (lines 1981-1998):
    - Creates `FuturesUnordered` stream of probe tasks
    - Each task: `run_single_probe()` with semaphore permits

  - **Result Processing** (lines 2000-2184):
    - **Supported** (2048-2092): Clears any previous unsupported flag, broadcasts model visibility update
    - **Unsupported** (2093-2145): Marks model as unsupported with detail/provider reason
    - **Error** (2147-2162): Records error per provider
    - Broadcasts incremental progress after each model completes

### **4. Single Model Probe**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/chat/src/lib.rs`

- **Function** `run_single_probe()` (lines 589-726):
  - Calls `provider.probe()` with 20-second timeout
  - **Probe Method** (agents/src/model.rs lines 443-465): Default implementation sends "ping" message, returns on first Delta/Done event
  - Handles rate-limit backoff tracking per provider
  - Returns `ProbeOutcome` with status: `Supported`, `Unsupported{detail, provider}`, or `Error{message}`

### **5. Model Discovery (Initialization)**

Models are discovered in two phases:

**Phase 1: Parallel Startup Discovery**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/providers/src/lib.rs`

- **Function** `ProviderRegistry::fire_discoveries()` (lines 1515-1641):
  - Spawns background threads for model discovery from `/v1/models` endpoints
  - For **OpenAI-compatible providers** (lines 1538-1592):
    - Checks `supports_model_discovery` flag or `fetch_models` user opt-in
    - Calls `openai::start_model_discovery(api_key, base_url)` for most providers
    - Special case for Ollama: calls `start_ollama_discovery()`
    - Custom providers also use `openai::start_model_discovery()`

**Phase 2: Model Discovery Implementation**

**File**: `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/providers/src/openai.rs`

- **Function** `fetch_models_from_api()` (lines 354-380):
  - Makes HTTP GET to `{base_url}/models` endpoint
  - 8-second timeout (line 361)
  - Adds Bearer token in Authorization header
  - Parses JSON response via `parse_models_payload()`
  - Returns empty error if no models found
  
- **Function** `start_model_discovery()` (lines 384-398):
  - Spawns OS thread with tokio runtime
  - Runs `fetch_models_from_api()` asynchronously
  - Sends result via sync_channel
  - Non-blocking from caller's perspective
  
- **Endpoint Path** (line 48): `const OPENAI_MODELS_ENDPOINT_PATH: &str = "/models";`
  - Combined with base_url in `models_endpoint()` function (line 343-347)

- **Discovery Integration** (lines 2251-2262):
  - Pre-fetched models merged with configured preferred models
  - Falls back to static catalog if discovery fails

### **6. Key Data Structures**

- **ProbeOutcome** (chat/src/lib.rs): `{ model_id, display_name, provider_name, status }`
- **DiscoveredModel** (providers/src/lib.rs line 56): Model discovered from API
- **ModelInfo**: Registered model with provider, display name, timestamps
- **Broadcast Payload**: JSON with phase, progress stats, error details

### **Key Flow for OpenAI-Compatible Detection:**

1. Frontend: User clicks "Detect All Models" → RPC `models.detect_supported` 
2. Backend: Collects all enabled models from provider registry
3. Concurrent Probing: Each model sends "ping" via `provider.probe()` → streams to `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint
4. Result Recording: Model marked supported/unsupported based on probe success
5. WebSocket Broadcasts: Real-time progress updates with `models.updated` events
6. Frontend Updates: UI shows progress bar, counts, and final summary

### **Important Files & Functions:**

1. `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/web/src/assets/js/page-providers.js` - Frontend UI (lines 117-151)
2. `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/chat/src/lib.rs` - `detect_supported` impl (lines 1883-2224), `run_single_probe` (lines 589-726)
3. `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/gateway/src/methods/services.rs` - RPC registration (lines 3158-3169)
4. `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/providers/src/openai.rs` - Model discovery (lines 354-398)
5. `/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/providers/src/lib.rs` - Provider registry & discovery startup (lines 1515-1641, 2182-2333)

  1516→        config: &ProvidersConfig,
  1517→        env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,

  1643→    /// Drain all pending discovery receivers (blocking on each `recv()`).

  1645→    /// Returns a map from provider name to discovered models.
  1646→    pub fn collect_discoveries(
  1647→        pending: PendingDiscoveries,
  1648→    ) -> HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>> {
  1649→        let mut results: HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>> = HashMap::new();
  1650→        for (key, rx) in pending {
  1651→            match rx.recv() {
  1652→                Ok(Ok(models)) => {
  1653→                    tracing::debug!(
  1654→                        provider = %key,
  1655→                        model_count = models.len(),
  1656→                        "parallel model discovery succeeded"
  1657→                    );
  1658→                    results.insert(key, models);
  1659→                },
  1660→                Ok(Err(err)) => {
  1661→                    let msg = err.to_string();
  1662→                    if msg.contains("not logged in")
  1663→                        || msg.contains("tokens not found")
  1664→                        || msg.contains("not configured")
  1665→                    {
  1666→                        tracing::debug!(
  1667→                            provider = %key,
  1668→                            error = %err,
  1669→                            "provider not configured, skipping model discovery"
  1670→                        );
  1671→                    } else {
  1672→                        tracing::warn!(
  1673→                            provider = %key,
  1674→                            error = %err,
  1675→                            "parallel model discovery failed"
  1676→                        );
  1677→                    }
  1678→                },
  1679→                Err(err) => {
  1680→                    tracing::warn!(
  1681→                        provider = %key,
  1682→                        error = %err,
  1683→                        "parallel model discovery worker crashed"
  1684→                    );
  1685→                },
  1686→            }
  1687→        }
  1688→        results
  1689→    }

  1715→        for &(env_key, provider_name, default_model_id, display_name) in genai_models {
  1716→            if !config.is_enabled(provider_name) {

  1720→            // Use config api_key or fall back to env var.

  1722→            else {

  1724→            };
  1725→
  1726→            let model_id = configured_models_for_provider(config, provider_name)
  1727→                .into_iter()
  1728→                .next()
  1729→                .unwrap_or_else(|| default_model_id.to_string());
  1730→
  1731→            // Get alias if configured (for metrics differentiation).
  1732→            let alias = config.get(provider_name).and_then(|e| e.alias.clone());
  1733→            let genai_provider_name = alias.unwrap_or_else(|| format!("genai/{provider_name}"));
  1734→            if self.has_model_any_provider(&model_id) {
  1735→                continue;
  1736→            }
  1737→
  1738→            let provider = Arc::new(genai_provider::GenaiProvider::new(
  1739→                model_id.clone(),
  1740→                genai_provider_name.clone(),

  1742→            ));

   261→fn merge_discovered_with_fallback_catalog(
   262→    discovered: Vec<DiscoveredModel>,
   263→    fallback: Vec<DiscoveredModel>,
   264→) -> Vec<DiscoveredModel> {
   265→    if discovered.is_empty() {
   266→        return fallback;
   267→    }
   268→
   269→    let fallback_by_id: HashMap<String, DiscoveredModel> =
   270→        fallback.into_iter().map(|m| (m.id.clone(), m)).collect();
   271→    discovered
   272→        .into_iter()
   273→        .map(|m| {
   274→            let display_name = if m.display_name.trim().is_empty() {
   275→                fallback_by_id
   276→                    .get(&m.id)
   277→                    .map(|fb| fb.display_name.clone())
   278→                    .unwrap_or_else(|| m.id.clone())
   279→            } else {
   280→                m.display_name
   281→            };
   282→            DiscoveredModel {
   283→                id: m.id,
   284→                display_name,
   285→                created_at: m.created_at,
   286→                recommended: m.recommended,
   287→            }
   288→        })
   289→        .collect()
   290→}
   291→
   292→fn normalize_ollama_api_base_url(base_url: &str) -> String {
   293→    let trimmed = base_url.trim_end_matches('/');
   294→    trimmed.strip_suffix("/v1").unwrap_or(trimmed).to_string()
   295→}
   296→
   297→/// Parse `Retry-After` header as milliseconds.
   298→///
   299→/// `Retry-After` may be either delta-seconds or an HTTP date. We currently
   300→/// consume delta-seconds, which is what providers typically return for 429.

  1314→    fn refresh_dynamic_source_models(
  1315→        &mut self,
  1316→        source: &dyn DynamicModelDiscovery,
  1317→        config: &ProvidersConfig,
  1318→    ) -> bool {
  1319→        if !source.is_enabled_and_authenticated(config) {
  1320→            return false;
  1321→        }
  1322→        if !source.should_fetch_models(config) {
  1323→            return false;
  1324→        }
  1325→
  1326→        let live_catalog = match source.live_models() {
  1327→            Ok(models) => models,
  1328→            Err(err) => {
  1329→                tracing::warn!(
  1330→                    provider = source.provider_name(),
  1331→                    error = %err,
  1332→                    "skipping dynamic model refresh because live fetch failed"
  1333→                );
  1334→                return false;
  1335→            },
  1336→        };
  1337→
  1338→        let Some(next_models) =
  1339→            Self::desired_models_for_dynamic_source(source, config, live_catalog)
  1340→        else {
  1341→            return false;
  1342→        };
  1343→
  1344→        let new_entries: Vec<(ModelInfo, Arc<dyn LlmProvider>)> = next_models
  1345→            .into_iter()
  1346→            .map(|model| {
  1347→                (
  1348→                    ModelInfo {
  1349→                        id: model.id.clone(),
  1350→                        provider: source.provider_name().to_string(),
  1351→                        display_name: source.display_name(&model.id, &model.display_name),
  1352→                        created_at: model.created_at,
  1353→                        recommended: model.recommended,
  1354→                    },
  1355→                    source.build_provider(model.id, config),
  1356→                )
  1357→            })
  1358→            .collect();
  1359→
  1360→        // Replace stale provider entries atomically only after successful fetch.
  1361→        let stale_ids: Vec<String> = self
  1362→            .models
  1363→            .iter()
  1364→            .filter(|m| m.provider == source.provider_name())
  1365→            .map(|m| m.id.clone())
  1366→            .collect();
  1367→        for model_id in &stale_ids {
  1368→            self.providers.remove(model_id);
  1369→        }
  1370→        self.models.retain(|m| m.provider != source.provider_name());
  1371→        for (info, provider) in new_entries {
  1372→            self.register(info, provider);
  1373→        }

   343→fn models_endpoint(base_url: &str) -> String {
   344→    format!(
   345→        "{}{OPENAI_MODELS_ENDPOINT_PATH}",
   346→        base_url.trim_end_matches('/')
   347→    )
   348→}
   349→
   350→/// Resolve the output index from a Responses API WebSocket streaming event.
   351→///
   352→/// The Responses API includes `output_index` on most events. Falls back to
   353→/// `item_index` / `index` for robustness, then to `fallback`.
   354→pub async fn fetch_models_from_api(
   355→    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
   356→    base_url: String,
   357→) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>> {
   358→    let client = crate::shared_http_client();
   359→    let response = client
   360→        .get(models_endpoint(&base_url))
   361→        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))
   362→        .header(
   363→            "Authorization",
   364→            format!("Bearer {}", api_key.expose_secret()),
   365→        )
   366→        .header("Accept", "application/json")
   367→        .send()
   368→        .await?;
   369→    let status = response.status();
   370→    let body = response.text().await?;
   371→    if !status.is_success() {
   372→        anyhow::bail!("openai models API error HTTP {status}");
   373→    }
   374→    let payload: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)?;
   375→    let models = parse_models_payload(&payload);
   376→    if models.is_empty() {
   377→        anyhow::bail!("openai models API returned no models");
   378→    }
   379→    Ok(models)
   380→}
   381→
   382→/// Spawn model discovery in a background thread and return the receiver
   383→/// immediately, without blocking. Call `.recv()` later to collect the result.
   384→pub fn start_model_discovery(
   385→    api_key: secrecy::Secret<String>,
   386→    base_url: String,
   387→) -> mpsc::Receiver<anyhow::Result<Vec<super::DiscoveredModel>>> {
   388→    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   389→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   390→        let result = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
   391→            .enable_all()
   392→            .build()
   393→            .map_err(anyhow::Error::from)
   394→            .and_then(|rt| rt.block_on(fetch_models_from_api(api_key, base_url)));
   395→        let _ = tx.send(result);
   396→    });
   397→    rx
   398→}

Now I have a complete picture. Let me write the analysis and plan.

---

## Analysis: Issue #551 — "Detect All Models" doesn't discover new models

### Root Cause

The "Detect All Models" button (`models.detect_supported` RPC) only **probes** models already registered in the `ProviderRegistry`. It does NOT re-run model discovery (calling `/v1/models`) first.

The flow:

1. **At startup**: `ProviderRegistry::fire_discoveries()` calls `/v1/models` for each provider, discovered models get registered via `register_openai_compatible_providers()` — this is the only time `/v1/models` is called.

2. **When "Detect All Models" is clicked**: `detect_supported()` at `crates/chat/src/lib.rs:1939-1957` reads `reg.list_models()` and probes each one. No new models are discovered — it just checks whether the already-registered models respond to a ping.

So if the user adds a new model to their llama.cpp backend after Moltis starts, "Detect All Models" will never find it because it never re-queries `/v1/models`.

### Why it regressed

The reporter says this worked in 0.10.18. Likely the earlier version either (a) re-ran discovery as part of detection, or (b) had a different flow that called `/v1/models` on each detection. The current architecture split discovery (startup-only) from detection (probe-only).

### Fix Plan

**Goal**: Before probing, re-run model discovery for the relevant providers, merge newly discovered models into the registry, then proceed with probing.

**Approach**: Add a "rediscovery" step at the top of `detect_supported()`:

1. **In `ProviderRegistry`** (`crates/providers/src/lib.rs`):
   - Add a new public async method `rediscover_models(&mut self, config, env_overrides, provider_filter)` that:
     - Calls `fire_discoveries()` (or a targeted subset for a specific provider filter)
     - Collects results via `collect_discoveries()`
     - For each provider with new models, registers any models not already in the registry (similar to what `register_openai_compatible_providers` does, but incremental — only adding new models, not replacing)
   - This needs access to the `ProvidersConfig` and `env_overrides` to know API keys and base URLs

2. **In `LiveModelService`** (`crates/chat/src/lib.rs`):
   - Store a reference to the config (or a way to get it) so `detect_supported()` can call rediscovery
   - At the start of `detect_supported()`, before the probe loop (before line 1939), call the rediscovery method on the provider registry
   - After rediscovery, broadcast a `catalog` phase update so the frontend refreshes the model list
   - Then proceed with probing as before

3. **Considerations**:
   - The `provider_filter` param should scope rediscovery to a single provider when specified
   - `fire_discoveries` spawns OS threads with `block_on` — in the async `detect_supported` context, we should use `fetch_models_from_api` directly (it's already async) rather than the thread-spawning `start_model_discovery`
   - Rate limiting: rediscovery adds a network call per provider, but since this is user-initiated ("manual" reason), that's acceptable
   - The existing `refresh_dynamic_source_models` pattern for OAuth providers (Codex, Copilot) is a good reference for how to incrementally add models

**Key files to modify**:
- `crates/providers/src/lib.rs` — add incremental rediscovery method on `ProviderRegistry`
- `crates/chat/src/lib.rs` — call rediscovery before probing in `detect_supported()`
- Possibly `crates/providers/src/openai.rs` — if we need an async-native discovery path (already have `fetch_models_from_api`)

**Tests needed**:
- Unit test: rediscovery adds newly found models to registry
- Unit test: rediscovery doesn't duplicate existing models
- Integration test: detect_supported finds models added after initial startup

Want me to proceed with implementation?

Now I have a thorough understanding. Let me design the implementation approach.

The cleanest approach is to add an async `rediscover_provider_models` method on `ProviderRegistry` that:
1. Takes config + env_overrides + optional provider filter
2. Calls `fetch_models_from_api` (async, already exists) for matching providers  
3. Registers any newly discovered models that aren't already in the registry
4. Returns the count of new models added

Then in `detect_supported()`, call this before the probe loop. The `LiveModelService` needs config + env_overrides stored.

Let me start implementing.

Now I have everything I need. Let me implement the solution. The approach:

1. **Add `rediscover_models` async method on `ProviderRegistry`** — fetches `/v1/models` for OpenAI-compat and custom providers, registers new models
2. **Add config/env_overrides to `LiveModelService`** — so it can call rediscovery
3. **Call rediscovery at the top of `detect_supported()`** — before probing

  1377→
  1378→    /// Register a provider manually.
  1379→    pub fn register(&mut self, mut info: ModelInfo, provider: Arc<dyn LlmProvider>) {
  1380→        let model_id = raw_model_id(&info.id).to_string();
  1381→        let registry_model_id = namespaced_model_id(&info.provider, &model_id);
  1382→        info.id = registry_model_id.clone();
  1383→        let wrapped: Arc<dyn LlmProvider> = Arc::new(RegistryModelProvider {
  1384→            model_id: registry_model_id.clone(),
  1385→            inner: provider,
  1386→        });
  1387→        self.providers.insert(registry_model_id, wrapped);
  1388→        self.models.push(info);
  1389→    }
  1390→
  1391→    /// Unregister a provider by model ID. Returns true if it was removed.
  1392→    pub fn unregister(&mut self, model_id: &str) -> bool {
  1393→        let resolved_id = self.resolve_registry_model_id(model_id, None);
  1394→        let removed = resolved_id
  1395→            .as_deref()
  1396→            .and_then(|id| self.providers.remove(id))
  1397→            .is_some();
  1398→        if removed && let Some(id) = resolved_id {
  1399→            self.models.retain(|m| m.id != id);
  1400→        }
  1401→        removed
  1402→    }

  2335→    /// Register custom OpenAI-compatible providers (names starting with `custom-`).
  2336→    /// These are user-added endpoints that may support model discovery via `/v1/models`.
  2337→    fn register_custom_providers(
  2338→        &mut self,
  2339→        config: &ProvidersConfig,
  2340→        prefetched: &HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>>,
  2341→    ) {
  2342→        for (name, entry) in &config.providers {
  2343→            if !name.starts_with("custom-") || !entry.enabled {
  2344→                continue;
  2345→            }
  2346→
  2347→            let Some(api_key) = entry
  2348→                .api_key
  2349→                .as_ref()
  2350→                .filter(|k| !k.expose_secret().is_empty())
  2351→            else {
  2352→                continue;
  2353→            };
  2354→
  2355→            let Some(base_url) = entry.base_url.as_ref().filter(|u| !u.trim().is_empty()) else {
  2356→                continue;
  2357→            };
  2358→
  2359→            let preferred = configured_models_for_provider(config, name);
  2360→
  2361→            // Use pre-fetched results from parallel discovery.
  2362→            let discovered = if should_fetch_models(config, name) {
  2363→                match prefetched.get(name.as_str()) {
  2364→                    Some(models) => models.clone(),
  2365→                    None => Vec::new(),
  2366→                }
  2367→            } else {
  2368→                Vec::new()
  2369→            };
  2370→
  2371→            let models = merge_preferred_and_discovered_models(preferred, discovered);
  2372→            if models.is_empty() {
  2373→                tracing::debug!(
  2374→                    provider = %name,
  2375→                    "custom provider has no models — skipping registration"
  2376→                );
  2377→                continue;
  2378→            }
  2379→
  2380→            let custom_tool_mode = entry.tool_mode;
  2381→            for model in models {
  2382→                let (model_id, display_name, created_at, recommended) = (
  2383→                    model.id,
  2384→                    model.display_name,
  2385→                    model.created_at,
  2386→                    model.recommended,
  2387→                );
  2388→                if self.has_provider_model(name, &model_id) {
  2389→                    continue;
  2390→                }
  2391→                let mut oai = openai::OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(
  2392→                    api_key.clone(),
  2393→                    model_id.clone(),
  2394→                    base_url.clone(),
  2395→                    name.clone(),
  2396→                )
  2397→                .with_stream_transport(entry.stream_transport);
  2398→                if !matches!(entry.wire_api, moltis_config::WireApi::ChatCompletions) {
  2399→                    oai = oai.with_wire_api(entry.wire_api);
  2400→                }
  2401→                if !matches!(custom_tool_mode, moltis_config::ToolMode::Auto) {
  2402→                    oai = oai.with_tool_mode(custom_tool_mode);
  2403→                }
  2404→                let provider = Arc::new(oai);
  2405→                self.register(
  2406→                    ModelInfo {
  2407→                        id: model_id,
  2408→                        provider: name.clone(),
  2409→                        display_name,
  2410→                        created_at,
  2411→                        recommended,
  2412→                    },
  2413→                    provider,
  2414→                );

  2057→        // Anthropic — register all known Claude models when API key is available.

  2067→

   353→}
   354→
   355→/// Spawn Ollama model discovery in a background thread and return the receiver
   356→/// immediately, without blocking. Call `.recv()` later to collect the result.
   357→fn start_ollama_discovery(
   358→    base_url: &str,
   359→) -> std::sync::mpsc::Receiver<anyhow::Result<Vec<DiscoveredModel>>> {
   360→    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   361→    let base_url = base_url.to_string();
   362→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   363→        let result = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
   364→            .enable_all()
   365→            .build()
   366→            .map_err(anyhow::Error::from)
   367→            .and_then(|rt| rt.block_on(discover_ollama_models_from_api(base_url)));
   368→        let _ = tx.send(result);
   369→    });
   370→    rx
   371→}
   372→

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "    rx\n}\n\n// ── Ollama model info probing ────────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "    rx\n}\n\n/// Asynchronously fetch models from all discoverable provider APIs.\n///\n/// Runs `/v1/models` (or Ollama `/api/tags`) for each eligible provider\n/// concurrently and returns the results keyed by provider config name.\n/// Failures for individual providers are logged and skipped.\n///\n/// `provider_filter` narrows the scope to a single provider name (case-\n/// insensitive comparison against config name or alias).\npub async fn fetch_discoverable_models(\n    config: &ProvidersConfig,\n    env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,\n    provider_filter: Option<&str>,\n) -> HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>> {\n    use futures::future::join_all;\n\n    let filter_matches =\n        |name: &str| -> bool { provider_filter.is_none_or(|f| f.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)) };\n\n    let mut tasks: Vec<(\n        String,\n        Pin<Box<dyn std::future::Future<Output = anyhow::Result<Vec<DiscoveredModel>>> + Send>>,\n    )> = Vec::new();\n\n    // ── OpenAI builtin ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    if filter_matches(\"openai\")\n        && config.is_enabled(\"openai\")\n        && !cfg!(test)\n        && let Some(key) = resolve_api_key(config, \"openai\", \"OPENAI_API_KEY\", env_overrides)\n        && should_fetch_models(config, \"openai\")\n    {\n        let base_url = config\n            .get(\"openai\")\n            .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())\n            .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, \"OPENAI_BASE_URL\"))\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| \"https://api.openai.com/v1\".into());\n        tasks.push((\"openai\".into(), Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(key, base_url))));\n    }\n\n    // ── OpenAI-compatible providers ───────────────────────────────────\n    for def in OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDERS {\n        if !filter_matches(def.config_name) || !config.is_enabled(def.config_name) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let key = resolve_api_key(config, def.config_name, def.env_key, env_overrides);\n        let key = if !def.requires_api_key {\n            key.or_else(|| Some(secrecy::Secret::new(def.config_name.into())))\n        } else if def.config_name == \"gemini\" {\n            key.or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, \"GOOGLE_API_KEY\").map(secrecy::Secret::new))\n        } else {\n            key\n        };\n        let Some(key) = key else {\n            continue;\n        };\n\n        let base_url = config\n            .get(def.config_name)\n            .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())\n            .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, def.env_base_url_key))\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| def.default_base_url.into());\n\n        if def.local_only {\n            let has_explicit_entry = config.get(def.config_name).is_some();\n            let has_env_base_url = env_value(env_overrides, def.env_base_url_key).is_some();\n            let preferred = configured_models_for_provider(config, def.config_name);\n            if !has_explicit_entry && !has_env_base_url && preferred.is_empty() {\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n\n        let user_opted_in = config\n            .get(def.config_name)\n            .is_some_and(|entry| entry.fetch_models);\n        let try_fetch = def.supports_model_discovery || user_opted_in;\n        if !try_fetch || !should_fetch_models(config, def.config_name) {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if def.config_name == \"ollama\" {\n            tasks.push((\n                def.config_name.into(),\n                Box::pin(discover_ollama_models_from_api(base_url)),\n            ));\n        } else {\n            tasks.push((\n                def.config_name.into(),\n                Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(key, base_url)),\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ── Custom providers ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n    for (name, entry) in &config.providers {\n        if !name.starts_with(\"custom-\") || !entry.enabled {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if !filter_matches(name) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let Some(api_key) = entry\n            .api_key\n            .as_ref()\n            .filter(|k| !k.expose_secret().is_empty())\n        else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        let Some(base_url) = entry.base_url.as_ref().filter(|u| !u.trim().is_empty()) else {\n            continue;\n        };\n        if should_fetch_models(config, name) {\n            tasks.push((\n                name.clone(),\n                Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(\n                    api_key.clone(),\n                    base_url.clone(),\n                )),\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Run all fetches concurrently.\n    let names: Vec<String> = tasks.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();\n    let futures: Vec<_> = tasks.into_iter().map(|(_, fut)| fut).collect();\n    let results = join_all(futures).await;\n\n    let mut map = HashMap::new();\n    for (name, result) in names.into_iter().zip(results) {\n        match result {\n            Ok(models) => {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    provider = %name,\n                    model_count = models.len(),\n                    \"runtime model rediscovery succeeded\"\n                );\n                map.insert(name, models);\n            },\n            Err(err) => {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    provider = %name,\n                    error = %err,\n                    \"runtime model rediscovery failed\"\n                );\n            },\n        }\n    }\n    map\n}\n\n// ── Ollama model info probing ────────────────────────────────────────────────"
}

\"custom-test\".to_string(),\n            moltis_config::schema::ProviderEntry {\n                enabled: true,\n                api_key: Some(secrecy::Secret::new(\"test-key\".into())),\n > AGENT

The issue: `reg.rediscover_models(...)` holds the write lock across the HTTP fetches (because `rediscover_models` calls `fetch_discoverable_models` internally, which is async). Let me refactor to split the fetch and registration phases so the lock is only held during the fast registration.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Qqnk8bTkAm7Z34yXvf7dxJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/cut-holiday/crates/chat/src/lib.rs",
  "old_string": "        // Phase 0: re-discover models from provider APIs so that newly\n        // added models (e.g. a model loaded into llama.cpp after startup)\n        // are found before probing. Fetches run outside the registry lock.\n        {\n            let new_count = {\n                let mut reg = self.providers.write().await;\n                reg.rediscover_models(\n                    &self.providers_config,\n                    &self.env_overrides,\n                    provider_filter.as_deref(),\n                )\n                .await\n            };\n            if new_count > 0 {\n                tracing::info!(\n                    new_models = new_count,\n                    \"rediscovery registered new models before probe\"\n                );\n            }\n        }",
  "new_string": "        // Phase 0: re-discover models from provider APIs so that newly\n        // added models (e.g. a model loaded into llama.cpp after startup)\n        // are found before probing.\n        //\n        // HTTP fetches run outside the registry lock; only the fast\n        // in-memory registration takes the write lock.\n        {\n            let fetched = moltis_providers::fetch_discoverable_models(\n                &self.providers_config,\n                &self.env_overrides,\n                provider_filter.as_deref(),\n            )\n            .await;\n            if !fetched.is_empty() {\n                let mut reg = self.providers.write().await;\n                let new_count = reg.register_rediscovered_models(\n                    &self.providers_config,\n                    &self.env_overrides,\n                    &fetched,\n                );\n                if new_count > 0 {\n                    tracing::info!(\n                        new_models = new_count,\n                        \"rediscovery registered new models before probe\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }"
}

+/// Asynchronously fetch models from all discoverable provider APIs.
+///
+/// Runs `/v1/models` (or Ollama `/api/tags`) for each eligible provider
+/// concurrently and returns the results keyed by provider config name.
+/// Failures for individual providers are logged and skipped.
+///
+/// `provider_filter` narrows the scope to a single provider name (case-
+/// insensitive comparison against config name or alias).
+pub async fn fetch_discoverable_models(
+    config: &ProvidersConfig,
+    env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,
+    provider_filter: Option<&str>,
+) -> HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>> {
+    use futures::future::join_all;
+
+    let filter_matches =
+        |name: &str| -> bool { provider_filter.is_none_or(|f| f.eq_ignore_ascii_case(name)) };
+
+    let mut tasks: Vec<(
+        String,
+        Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = anyhow::Result<Vec<DiscoveredModel>>> + Send>>,
+    )> = Vec::new();
+
+    // ── OpenAI builtin ────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    if filter_matches("openai")
+        && config.is_enabled("openai")
+        && !cfg!(test)
+        && let Some(key) = resolve_api_key(config, "openai", "OPENAI_API_KEY", env_overrides)
+        && should_fetch_models(config, "openai")
+    {
+        let base_url = config
+            .get("openai")
+            .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
+            .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "OPENAI_BASE_URL"))
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| "https://api.openai.com/v1".into());
+        tasks.push((
+            "openai".into(),
+            Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(key, base_url)),
+        ));
+    }
+
+    // ── OpenAI-compatible providers ───────────────────────────────────
+    for def in OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDERS {
+        if !filter_matches(def.config_name) || !config.is_enabled(def.config_name) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        let key = resolve_api_key(config, def.config_name, def.env_key, env_overrides);
+        let key = if !def.requires_api_key {
+            key.or_else(|| Some(secrecy::Secret::new(def.config_name.into())))
+        } else if def.config_name == "gemini" {
+            key.or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "GOOGLE_API_KEY").map(secrecy::Secret::new))
+        } else {
+            key
+        };
+        let Some(key) = key else {
+            continue;
+        };
+
+        let base_url = config
+            .get(def.config_name)
+            .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
+            .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, def.env_base_url_key))
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| def.default_base_url.into());
+
+        if def.local_only {
+            let has_explicit_entry = config.get(def.config_name).is_some();
+            let has_env_base_url = env_value(env_overrides, def.env_base_url_key).is_some();
+            let preferred = configured_models_for_provider(config, def.config_name);
+            if !has_explicit_entry && !has_env_base_url && preferred.is_empty() {
+                continue;
+            }
+        }
+
+        let user_opted_in = config
+            .get(def.config_name)
+            .is_some_and(|entry| entry.fetch_models);
+        let try_fetch = def.supports_model_discovery || user_opted_in;
+        if !try_fetch || !should_fetch_models(config, def.config_name) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if def.config_name == "ollama" {
+            tasks.push((
+                def.config_name.into(),
+                Box::pin(discover_ollama_models_from_api(base_url)),
+            ));
+        } else {
+            tasks.push((
+                def.config_name.into(),
+                Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(key, base_url)),
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+
+    // ── Custom providers ──────────────────────────────────────────────
+    for (name, entry) in &config.providers {
+        if !name.starts_with("custom-") || !entry.enabled {
+            continue;
+        }
+        if !filter_matches(name) {
+            continue;
+        }
+        let Some(api_key) = entry
+            .api_key
+            .as_ref()
+            .filter(|k| !k.expose_secret().is_empty())
+        else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        let Some(base_url) = entry.base_url.as_ref().filter(|u| !u.trim().is_empty()) else {
+            continue;
+        };
+        if should_fetch_models(config, name) {
+            tasks.push((
+                name.clone(),
+                Box::pin(openai::fetch_models_from_api(
+                    api_key.clone(),
+                    base_url.clone(),
+                )),
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Run all fetches concurrently.
+    let names: Vec<String> = tasks.iter().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect();
+    let futures: Vec<_> = tasks.into_iter().map(|(_, fut)| fut).collect();
+    let results = join_all(futures).await;
+
+    let mut map = HashMap::new();
+    for (name, result) in names.into_iter().zip(results) {
+        match result {
+            Ok(models) => {
+                tracing::debug!(
+                    provider = %name,
+                    model_count = models.len(),
+                    "runtime model rediscovery succeeded"
+                );
+                map.insert(name, models);
+            },
+            Err(err) => {
+                tracing::debug!(
+                    provider = %name,
+                    error = %err,
+                    "runtime model rediscovery failed"
+                );
+            },
+        }
+    }
+    map
+}
+

+    /// Asynchronously re-discover models from provider APIs (`/v1/models`)
+    /// and register any newly found models that are not already present.
+    ///
+    /// This is the runtime counterpart of the startup discovery pipeline
+    /// (`fire_discoveries` → `collect_discoveries` → registration). Unlike
+    /// startup, HTTP fetches run fully async and only new models are added.
+    ///
+    /// Returns the number of newly registered models.
+    pub async fn rediscover_models(
+        &mut self,
+        config: &ProvidersConfig,
+        env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,
+        provider_filter: Option<&str>,
+    ) -> usize {
+        let fetched = fetch_discoverable_models(config, env_overrides, provider_filter).await;
+        self.register_rediscovered_models(config, env_overrides, &fetched)
+    }
+
+    /// Register models from a rediscovery fetch, skipping those already present.
+    /// Returns the number of newly registered models.
+    pub fn register_rediscovered_models(
+        &mut self,
+        config: &ProvidersConfig,
+        env_overrides: &HashMap<String, String>,
+        fetched: &HashMap<String, Vec<DiscoveredModel>>,
+    ) -> usize {
+        let mut added = 0usize;
+
+        // ── OpenAI builtin ────────────────────────────────────────────
+        if let Some(models) = fetched.get("openai")
+            && config.is_enabled("openai")
+            && let Some(key) = resolve_api_key(config, "openai", "OPENAI_API_KEY", env_overrides)
+        {
+            let base_url = config
+                .get("openai")
+                .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
+                .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "OPENAI_BASE_URL"))
+                .unwrap_or_else(|| "https://api.openai.com/v1".into());
+            let alias = config.get("openai").and_then(|e| e.alias.clone());
+            let provider_label = alias.unwrap_or_else(|| "openai".into());
+            let stream_transport = config
+                .get("openai")
+                .map(|entry| entry.stream_transport)
+                .unwrap_or(ProviderStreamTransport::Sse);
+
+            for model in models {
+                if self.has_provider_model(&provider_label, &model.id) {
+                    continue;
+                }
+                let provider = Arc::new(
+                    openai::OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(
+                        key.clone(),
+                        model.id.clone(),
+                        base_url.clone(),
+                        provider_label.clone(),
+                    )
+                    .with_stream_transport(stream_transport),
+                );
+                self.register(
+                    ModelInfo {
+                        id: model.id.clone(),
+                        provider: provider_label.clone(),
+                        display_name: model.display_name.clone(),
+                        created_at: model.created_at,
+                        recommended: model.recommended,
+                    },
+                    provider,
+                );
+                added += 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // ── OpenAI-compatible providers ───────────────────────────────
+        for def in OPENAI_COMPAT_PROVIDERS {
+            let Some(models) = fetched.get(def.config_name) else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            if !config.is_enabled(def.config_name) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            let key = resolve_api_key(config, def.config_name, def.env_key, env_overrides);
+            let key = if !def.requires_api_key {
+                key.or_else(|| Some(secrecy::Secret::new(def.config_name.into())))
+            } else if def.config_name == "gemini" {
+                key.or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, "GOOGLE_API_KEY").map(secrecy::Secret::new))
+            } else {
+                key
+            };
+            let Some(key) = key else {
+                continue;
+            };
+
+            let base_url = config
+                .get(def.config_name)
+                .and_then(|e| e.base_url.clone())
+                .or_else(|| env_value(env_overrides, def.env_base_url_key))
+                .unwrap_or_else(|| def.default_base_url.into());
+            let alias = config.get(def.config_name).and_then(|e| e.alias.clone());
+            let provider_label = alias.unwrap_or_else(|| def.config_name.into());
+            let stream_transport = config
+                .get(def.config_name)
+                .map(|entry| entry.stream_transport)
+                .unwrap_or(ProviderStreamTransport::Sse);
+            let cache_retention = config
+                .get(def.config_name)
+                .map(|e| e.cache_retention)
+                .unwrap_or(moltis_config::CacheRetention::Short);
+            let config_tool_mode = config
+                .get(def.config_name)
+                .map(|e| e.tool_mode)
+                .unwrap_or_default();
+            let is_ollama = def.config_name == "ollama";
+
+            // Batch-probe Ollama models for tool mode metadata.

+                probe_ollama_models_batch(&base_url, models)
+            } else {

+
+            for model in models {

+                } else {

+
+                let mut oai = openai::OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(

+                .with_stream_transport(stream_transport)

+
+                if !matches!(effective_tool_mode, moltis_config::ToolMode::Auto) {
+                    oai = oai.with_tool_mode(effective_tool_mode);
+                }
+
+                self.register(
+                    ModelInfo {
+                        id: model.id.clone(),
+                        provider: provider_label.clone(),
+                        display_name: model.display_name.clone(),
+                        created_at: model.created_at,
+                        recommended: model.recommended,
+                    },
+                    Arc::new(oai),
+                );
+                added += 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // ── Custom providers ──────────────────────────────────────────
+        for (name, entry) in &config.providers {
+            if !name.starts_with("custom-") || !entry.enabled {
+                continue;
+            }
+            let Some(models) = fetched.get(name.as_str()) else {
+                continue;
+            };
+            let Some(api_key) = entry

+                continue;
+            };
+            let Some(base_url) = entry.base_url.as_ref().filter(|u| !u.trim().is_empty()) else {
+                continue;
+            };

+
+            for model in models {

+                    continue;
+                }
+                let mut oai = openai::OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(

+                        recommended: model.recommended,
+                    },
+                    Arc::new(oai),

+    #[test]
+    fn register_rediscovered_models_adds_new_models() {
+        let mut config = ProvidersConfig::default();
+        config.providers.insert(
+            "custom-test".to_string(),
+            moltis_config::schema::ProviderEntry {
+                enabled: true,
+                api_key: Some(secrecy::Secret::new("test-key".into())),
+                base_url: Some("http://localhost:1234/v1".into()),
+                fetch_models: true,
+                ..Default::default()
+            },
+        );
+
+        let env_overrides = HashMap::new();
+
+        // Start with an empty registry (static catalogs only, no live fetch).
+        let mut reg = ProviderRegistry::from_config_with_static_catalogs(&config, &env_overrides);
+        let before = reg.list_models().len();
+
+        // Simulate a rediscovery that found two new models.
+        let mut fetched = HashMap::new();
+        fetched.insert("custom-test".to_string(), vec![
+            DiscoveredModel::new("new-model-a", "New Model A"),
+            DiscoveredModel::new("new-model-b", "New Model B"),
+        ]);
+
+        let added = reg.register_rediscovered_models(&config, &env_overrides, &fetched);
+        assert_eq!(added, 2, "should register 2 new models");
+        assert_eq!(
+            reg.list_models().len(),
+            before + 2,
+            "model list should grow by 2"
+        );
+
+        // Running again with the same models should not add duplicates.
+        let added_again = reg.register_rediscovered_models(&config, &env_overrides, &fetched);
+        assert_eq!(added_again, 0, "should not re-register existing models");
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn register_rediscovered_models_skips_existing() {
+        let mut config = ProvidersConfig::default();
+        config.providers.insert(
+            "custom-test".to_string(),
+            moltis_config::schema::ProviderEntry {
+                enabled: true,
+                api_key: Some(secrecy::Secret::new("test-key".into())),
+                base_url: Some("http://localhost:1234/v1".into()),
+                fetch_models: true,
+                models: vec!["existing-model".to_string()],
+                ..Default::default()
+            },
+        );
+
+        let env_overrides = HashMap::new();
+        let mut reg = ProviderRegistry::from_config_with_static_catalogs(&config, &env_overrides);
+        let before = reg.list_models().len();
+
+        // Rediscovery returns both the existing model and a new one.
+        let mut fetched = HashMap::new();
+        fetched.insert("custom-test".to_string(), vec![
+            DiscoveredModel::new("existing-model", "Existing Model"),
+            DiscoveredModel::new("brand-new-model", "Brand New Model"),
+        ]);
+
+        let added = reg.register_rediscovered_models(&config, &env_overrides, &fetched);
+        assert_eq!(added, 1, "should only add the brand-new model");
+        assert_eq!(reg.list_models().len(), before + 1);
+    }
 }

> AGENT

The diff looks clean and correct. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Summary

**Issue**: [#551](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/issues/551) — "Detect All Models" only probed already-registered models instead of discovering new ones from provider APIs.

**Root cause**: Model discovery (`/v1/models` calls) only happened at startup. The `detect_supported()` method just probed existing registry entries.

**Fix** — 3 files changed:

### `crates/providers/src/lib.rs` (+442 lines)
- **`fetch_discoverable_models()`** — New pub async function that queries `/v1/models` (or Ollama `/api/tags`) for all eligible providers concurrently. Mirrors the startup `fire_discoveries()` logic but runs async instead of spawning OS threads.
- **`ProviderRegistry::register_rediscovered_models()`** — New pub method that takes fetched results and registers any models not already in the registry. Handles OpenAI builtin, OpenAI-compat providers, and custom providers.
- **`ProviderRegistry::rediscover_models()`** — Convenience async method combining both phases.
- **2 unit tests** — Verify new models are added and duplicates are skipped.

### `crates/chat/src/lib.rs` (+47 lines)
- Added `providers_config` and `env_overrides` fields to `LiveModelService` with `with_discovery_config()` builder.
- Added "Phase 0" in `detect_supported()` before the probe loop: fetches models without holding the registry lock, then takes a write lock only for the fast in-memory registration.

### `crates/gateway/src/server.rs` (+2 lines)
- Wires `effective_providers` config and `config_env_overrides` into `LiveModelService` via `with_discovery_config()`.

href=\"#\"><img alt=\"P1\" src=\"https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p1.svg?v=7\" align=\"top\"></a> **Ollama probes block under the write lock**\n\nThe PR description states *\"HTTP fetches run async outside the registry lock; only fast in-memory registration takes the write lock\"*, but `probe_ollama_models_batch` is called here inside `register_rediscovered_models`, which is only ever invoked after the caller acquires the write lock (`self.providers.write().await` at `chat/src/lib.rs:1955`). `probe_ollama_models_batch` spawns a thread, builds its own tokio runtime, fires concurrent HTTP requests to Ollama `/api/show`, then blocks the calling thread on `rx.recv()` until all responses arrive — holding the write lock for the full duration and freezing all concurrent model reads.\n\nTo match the stated design, the Ollama probes should be moved outside the lock. One approach is to pre-fetch them alongside the model-list discovery and pass the results into a lock-free registration helper:\n\n```rust\n// Outside the lock: fetch model list AND Ollama show-probes\nlet fetched = moltis_providers::fetch_discoverable_models(…).await;\nlet ollama_probes = moltis_providers::prefetch_ollama_probes(&fetched, &ollama_base_url);\n\n// Inside the lock: only fast in-memory work\nlet mut reg = self.providers.write().await;\nreg.register_rediscovered_models_with_probes(&config, &env_overrides, &fetched, &ollama_probes);\n```\nAlternatively, accept probes-under-lock but update the comments to reflect the actual behaviour.","created_at":"2026-04-05T15:18:41Z","updated_at":"2026-04-05T15:18:44Z","html_url":"https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/pull/560#discussion_r3036981589","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/pulls/560","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/pulls/comments/3036981589"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/pull/560#discussion_r3036981589"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/pulls/560"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/pulls/comments/3036981589/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":1958,"original_start_line":1958,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":1963,"original_line":1963,"side":"RIGHT","author_association":"CONTRIBUTOR","original_position":290,"position":290,"subject_type":"line"},{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/moltis-org/moltis/pulls/comments/3036981604","pull_request_review_id":4059564590,"id":3036981604,"node_id":"PRRC_kwDOREW6tc61BKlk","diff_hunk":"@@    544→/// Model families known to support native OpenAI-style tool calling in Ollama.
   545→const OLLAMA_NATIVE_TOOL_FAMILIES: &[&str] = &[
   546→    "llama3.1",
   547→    "llama3.2",

   559→/// Determine whether an Ollama model supports native tool calling based on its
   560→/// model name and family metadata from `/api/show`.
   561→fn ollama_model_supports_native_tools(model_name: &str, details: &OllamaModelDetails) -> bool {
   562→    let name_lower = model_name.to_ascii_lowercase();
   563→
   564→    // Check all family strings from the model details.
   565→    let families_iter = details
   566→        .family
   567→        .iter()
   568→        .chain(details.families.iter().flatten());
   569→    for family in families_iter {
   570→        let fam_lower = family.to_ascii_lowercase();
   571→        if OLLAMA_NATIVE_TOOL_FAMILIES
   572→            .iter()
   573→            .any(|known| fam_lower.contains(known))
   574→        {
   575→            return true;
   576→        }
   577→    }
   578→
   579→    // Heuristic: check model name for known families.
   580→    OLLAMA_NATIVE_TOOL_FAMILIES
   581→        .iter()
   582→        .any(|known| name_lower.contains(known))
   583→}
   584→
   585→/// Check if Ollama's `capabilities` list indicates native tool support.
   586→///
   587→/// Returns `Some(true)` if `"tools"` is present, `Some(false)` if capabilities
   588→/// exist but don't include tools, and `None` if the list is empty (pre-0.5.x
   589→/// Ollama versions that don't report capabilities).
   590→fn ollama_capabilities_support_tools(capabilities: &[String]) -> Option<bool> {
   591→    if capabilities.is_empty() {
   592→        return None;
   593→    }
   594→    Some(capabilities.iter().any(|c| c == "tools"))
   595→}

   599→async fn probe_ollama_model_info(
   600→    base_url: &str,
   601→    model_name: &str,
   602→) -> anyhow::Result<OllamaShowResponse> {
   603→    let api_base = normalize_ollama_api_base_url(base_url);
   604→    let endpoint = format!("{}/api/show", api_base.trim_end_matches('/'));
   605→    let response = reqwest::Client::builder()
   606→        .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3))
   607→        .build()?
   608→        .post(&endpoint)
   609→        .json(&serde_json::json!({ "name": model_name }))
   610→        .send()
   611→        .await?;
   612→
   613→    let status = response.status();
   614→    if !status.is_success() {
   615→        anyhow::bail!("ollama /api/show for {model_name} failed HTTP {status}");
   616→    }
   617→    Ok(response.json().await?)
   618→}
   619→
   620→/// Resolve the effective tool mode for an Ollama model.
   621→///
   622→/// - If the user configured an explicit `tool_mode`, use that.
   623→/// - Otherwise, check the model's `capabilities` list from Ollama (>= 0.5.x).
   624→/// - Fall back to the hardcoded family whitelist only when capabilities are
   625→///   unavailable (pre-0.5.x Ollama).
   626→fn resolve_ollama_tool_mode(
   627→    config_tool_mode: moltis_config::ToolMode,
   628→    model_name: &str,
   629→    probe_result: Option<&OllamaShowResponse>,
   630→) -> moltis_config::ToolMode {
   631→    use moltis_config::ToolMode;
   632→
   633→    match config_tool_mode {
   634→        ToolMode::Native | ToolMode::Text | ToolMode::Off => config_tool_mode,
   635→        ToolMode::Auto => {
   636→            // Prefer Ollama's own capabilities field when available.
   637→            if let Some(resp) = probe_result
   638→                && let Some(supports) = ollama_capabilities_support_tools(&resp.capabilities)
   639→            {
   640→                return if supports {
   641→                    ToolMode::Native
   642→                } else {
   643→                    ToolMode::Text
   644→                };
   645→            }
   646→            // Fallback: family whitelist (pre-0.5.x Ollama without capabilities).
   647→            let details = probe_result
   648→                .map(|r| &r.details)
   649→                .cloned()
   650→                .unwrap_or_default();
   651→            if ollama_model_supports_native_tools(model_name, &details) {
   652→                ToolMode::Native
   653→            } else {
   654→                ToolMode::Text
   655→            }
   656→        },
   657→    }
   658→}
   659→
   660→/// Batch-probe Ollama `/api/show` for a list of models.
   661→/// Runs probes in a dedicated thread with its own tokio runtime (same pattern
   662→/// as `discover_ollama_models`). Returns a map from model ID to show response;
   663→/// failures are silently dropped.
   664→fn probe_ollama_models_batch(
   665→    base_url: &str,
   666→    models: &[DiscoveredModel],
   667→) -> HashMap<String, OllamaShowResponse> {
   668→    if models.is_empty() {
   669→        return HashMap::new();
   670→    }
   671→    let base_url = base_url.to_string();
   672→    let model_ids: Vec<String> = models.iter().map(|m| m.id.clone()).collect();
   673→    let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel(1);
   674→
   675→    std::thread::spawn(move || {
   676→        let result = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
   677→            .enable_all()
   678→            .build()

Suitable for callers already in an async context\n/// (e.g. runtime rediscovery in `detect_supported`).\nasync fn probe_ollama_models_batch_async(\n    base_url: &str,\n    models: &[DiscoveredModel],\n) -> HashMap<String, OllamaShowResponse> {\n    if models.is_empty() {\n        return HashMap::new();\n    }\n    let futs: Vec<_> = models\n        .iter()\n        .map(|m| {\n            let base = base_url.to_string();\n            let model_id = m.id.clone();\n            async move {\n                let resp = probe_ollama_model_info(&base, &model_id).await;\n                (model_id, resp)\n            }\n        })\n        .collect();\n    futures::future::join_all(futs)\n        .await\n        .into_iter()\n        .filter_map(|(id, r)| r.ok().map(|resp| (id, resp)))\n        .collect()\n}\n\nstruct RegistryModelProvider {"

  "old_string": "pub struct LiveModelService {\n    providers: Arc<RwLock<ProviderRegistry>>,\n    disabled: Arc<RwLock<DisabledModelsStore>>,\n    state: Arc<OnceCell<Arc<dyn ChatRuntime>>>,\n    detect_gate: Arc<Semaphore>,\n    priority_models: Arc<RwLock<Vec<String>>>,\n    show_legacy_models: bool,\n    /// Provider config for runtime model rediscovery.\n    providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig,\n    /// Environment variable overrides for runtime model rediscovery.\n    env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,\n}\n\nimpl LiveModelService {\n    pub fn new(\n        providers: Arc<RwLock<ProviderRegistry>>,\n        disabled: Arc<RwLock<DisabledModelsStore>>,\n        priority_models: Vec<String>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            providers,\n            disabled,\n            state: Arc::new(OnceCell::new()),\n            detect_gate: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(1)),\n            priority_models: Arc::new(RwLock::new(priority_models)),\n            show_legacy_models: false,\n            providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig::default(),\n            env_overrides: HashMap::new(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn with_show_legacy_models(mut self, show: bool) -> Self {\n        self.show_legacy_models = show;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Set the provider config and env overrides used for runtime model\n    /// rediscovery when \"Detect All Models\" is triggered.\n    pub fn with_discovery_config(\n        mut self,\n        providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig,\n        env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        self.providers_config = providers_config;\n        self.env_overrides = env_overrides;\n        self\n    }",
  "new_string": "pub struct LiveModelService {\n    providers: Arc<RwLock<ProviderRegistry>>,\n    disabled: Arc<RwLock<DisabledModelsStore>>,\n    state: Arc<OnceCell<Arc<dyn ChatRuntime>>>,\n    detect_gate: Arc<Semaphore>,\n    priority_models: Arc<RwLock<Vec<String>>>,\n    show_legacy_models: bool,\n    /// Provider config for runtime model rediscovery.\n    providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig,\n    /// Environment variable overrides for runtime model rediscovery.\n    /// Shared so the gateway can update it after loading UI-stored keys.\n    env_overrides: Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, String>>>,\n}\n\nimpl LiveModelService {\n    pub fn new(\n        providers: Arc<RwLock<ProviderRegistry>>,\n        disabled: Arc<RwLock<DisabledModelsStore>>,\n        priority_models: Vec<String>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            providers,\n            disabled,\n            state: Arc::new(OnceCell::new()),\n            detect_gate: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(1)),\n            priority_models: Arc::new(RwLock::new(priority_models)),\n            show_legacy_models: false,\n            providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig::default(),\n            env_overrides: Arc::new(RwLock::new(HashMap::new())),\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn with_show_legacy_models(mut self, show: bool) -> Self {\n        self.show_legacy_models = show;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Set the provider config and initial env overrides used for runtime\n    /// model rediscovery when \"Detect All Models\" is triggered.\n    pub fn with_discovery_config(\n        mut self,\n        providers_config: moltis_config::schema::ProvidersConfig,\n        env_overrides: HashMap<String, String>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        self.providers_config = providers_config;\n        self.env_overrides = Arc::new(RwLock::new(env_overrides));\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Shared handle to the env overrides. Pass this to code that needs to\n    /// update the overrides after construction (e.g. when runtime UI-stored\n    /// API keys are loaded from the credential store).\n    pub fn env_overrides_handle(&self) -> Arc<RwLock<HashMap<String, String>>> {\n        Arc::clone(&self.env_overrides)\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "        // Phase 0: re-discover models from provider APIs so that newly\n        // added models (e.g. a model loaded into llama.cpp after startup)\n        // are found before probing.\n        //\n        // HTTP fetches and Ollama `/api/show` probes run outside the\n        // registry lock; only the fast in-memory registration takes it.\n        {\n            let env_snapshot = self.env_overrides.read().await.clone();\n            let result = moltis_providers::fetch_discoverable_models(\n                &self.providers_config,\n                &env_snapshot,\n                provider_filter.as_deref(),\n            )\n            .await;\n            if !result.is_empty() {\n                let mut reg = self.providers.write().await;\n                let new_count = reg.register_rediscovered_models(\n                    &self.providers_config,\n                    &env_snapshot,\n                    &result,\n                );\n                if new_count > 0 {\n                    tracing::info!(\n                        new_models = new_count,\n                        \"rediscovery registered new models before probe\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }"

> AGENT

Pushed. All three review comments addressed:

1. **Ollama probes outside lock** — Added `probe_ollama_models_batch_async` that runs in `fetch_discoverable_models` (outside the lock). Results bundled into `RediscoveryResult` so registration is pure in-memory work.

2. **Runtime UI-stored API keys** — Changed `env_overrides` to `Arc<RwLock<HashMap>>` with `env_overrides_handle()`. Gateway updates it at line 1652 after loading credential store, so "Detect All Models" sees UI-stored keys.

3. **Dead `rediscover_models` method** — Removed. Only the two-step pattern (fetch outside lock → register inside) remains.

> DEVELOPER

{"body":"\u003ch3\u003eGreptile Summary\u003c/h3\u003e\n\nThis PR fixes the \"Detect All Models\" feature so it re-queries each provider's `/v1/models` (or Ollama `/api/tags`) endpoint before probing, allowing models added to backends after startup to be discovered without a restart.\n\nKey changes:\n- New `fetch_discoverable_models` async function fetches model lists and Ollama `/api/show` probes concurrently, entirely outside any registry lock.\n- New `RediscoveryResult` bundles fetched models with Ollama probe data so `register_rediscovered_models` does only fast in-memory work while holding the write lock.\n- `LiveModelService.env_overrides` is promoted from a plain `HashMap` to `Arc\u003cRwLock\u003cHashMap\u003e\u003e` with an `env_overrides_handle()` accessor; the server wires in runtime UI-stored API keys after the credential store loads — resolving the prior gap where Settings-UI keys were invisible during rediscovery.\n- The previous `rediscover_models` convenience wrapper (which held `\u0026mut self` across I/O) is removed.\n- Unit tests cover the `register_rediscovered_models` add-new and skip-existing paths.\n\nAll three issues raised in the previous review round (Ollama probes under lock, UI-stored keys excluded, dead `rediscover_models` method) are fully resolved. One minor remaining nit: `RediscoveryResult::is_empty()` checks whether the provider map has any entries rather than whether any models were actually returned, so a provider that succeeds but returns an empty model list still triggers an unnecessary write-lock acquisition (no correctness impact).\n\n\u003ch3\u003eConfidence Score: 5/5\u003c/h3\u003e\n\nSafe to merge — all three prior P1 concerns are fully addressed; remaining finding is a minor P2 optimisation with no correctness impact\n\nLock-ordering fix, runtime-env integration, and dead-code removal are all correctly implemented and consistent with the stated design. The only open item is an is_empty() semantic mismatch that causes an occasional unnecessary write-lock acquisition but has zero impact on correctness or data integrity.\n\nNo files require special attention; crates/providers/src/lib.rs is the most complex change but is well-tested\n\n\u003ch3\u003eImportant Files Changed\u003c/h3\u003e\n\n\n\n\n| Filename | Overview |\n|----------|----------|\n| crates/providers/src/lib.rs | Adds fetch_discoverable_models (async/lock-free), probe_ollama_models_batch_async, RediscoveryResult bundle, and register_rediscovered_models (in-memory only); prior lock and dead-code issues resolved; minor is_empty() mismatch where an empty-success provider response causes unnecessary write-lock acquisition |\n| crates/chat/src/lib.rs | Adds Phase-0 rediscovery block in detect_supported: env snapshot taken outside lock, fetch runs fully async, write lock taken only for fast in-memory registration; promotes env_overrides to Arc\u003cRwLock\u003cHashMap\u003e\u003e with env_overrides_handle() accessor |\n| crates/gateway/src/server.rs | Wires env_overrides_handle() so runtime UI-stored API keys are merged into the model-service env after credential store loads, resolving the prior gap where Settings-UI keys were invisible during Detect All Models |\n\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\n\n\n\u003ch3\u003eSequence Diagram\u003c/h3\u003e\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant UI as Browser UI\n    participant MS as LiveModelService\n    participant Reg as ProviderRegistry\n    participant Prov as Provider APIs\n\n    UI-\u003e\u003eMS: detect_supported()\n    MS-\u003e\u003eMS: snapshot env_overrides (read lock, released)\n    MS-\u003e\u003eProv: fetch_discoverable_models() [async — NO registry lock]\n    note over Prov: /v1/models + Ollama /api/show probes\n    Prov--\u003e\u003eMS: RediscoveryResult {models, ollama_probes}\n    MS-\u003e\u003eReg: register_rediscovered_models() [write lock — in-memory only]\n    Reg--\u003e\u003eMS: new_count\n    note over Reg: write lock released\n    MS--\u003e\u003eUI: broadcast models.updated (catalog phase)\n    MS-\u003e\u003eReg: list_models() [read lock]\n    MS-\u003e\u003eProv: probe each model concurrently [NO lock]\n    Prov--\u003e\u003eMS: probe results\n    MS--\u003e\u003eUI: broadcast models.updated (results)\n```\n\n\u003c!-- greptile_other_comments_section --\u003e\n\n\u003csub\u003eReviews (2): Last reviewed commit: [\"fix(providers): address PR review — move...\"](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/commit/3f6351ef26420ca09410d65fe33eeda3f8ed678e) | [Re-trigger Greptile](https://app.greptile.com/api/retrigger?id=27398934)\u003c/sub\u003e","created_at":"2026-04-05T15:18:38Z","id":4189044877,"user":"greptile-apps[bot]"}
